MINISTRY OF LAW AND JUSTICE
(Legislative Department)

New Delhi, the 26th February, 2026
Phalguna 07, 1947 (Saka)

NOTIFICATION

S.O……(E).—In pursuance of section 12 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 (43
of 1951), the President is pleased to call upon the elected members of the Legislative Assembly of
each State specified in column (2) of the table below, to elect, in accordance with the provisions of
the said Act and of the rules and orders made thereunder, the number of members specified against
each State in column (3) of the said table, for the purpose of filling the seats of members of the
Council of States whose term of office is due to expire on the date mentioned in the corresponding
entry in column (4) of the said table, on the expiration of their term of office:—

Table

Serial Name of State No. of seats to be filled Date of expiry of term of office
number
1 2 3 4
1. Maharashtra 7
2. Odisha 4 02-04-2026
3. Tamil Nadu 6
4. West Bengal 5
5. Assam 3
6. Bihar 5
7. Chhattisgarh 2
09-04-2026
8. Haryana 2

9. Himachal Pradesh 1
10. Telangana 2

By order,

(AKALI V. KONGHAY)
Joint Secretary & Legislative Counsel.

ELECTION COMMISSION OF INDIA
Nirvachan Sadan, Ashoka Road, New Delhi – 110001

Dated: 26th February, 2026
Phalguna 07, 1947 (Saka)

NOTIFICATION

No. 318/CS-Multi/2026(1).—Whereas, the President of India has, by notification issued
under Section 12 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951(43 of 1951) and published in the
Gazette of India on 26th February, 2026 been pleased to call upon the elected members of each of
the Legislative Assemblies of the States, specified in column (1) of the TABLE below, to elect to
the Council of States, the number of member(s) specified against that State in column (2) of the
said TABLE:

TABLE

Sl. No. Name of State No. of members
1 2
1. Maharashtra 7
2. Odisha 4
3. Tamil Nadu 6
4. West Bengal 5
5. Assam 3
6. Bihar 5
7. Chhattisgarh 2
8. Haryana 2
9. Himachal Pradesh 1
10. Telangana 2

Now, therefore, in pursuance of sub-section (1) of section 39 and section 56 of the said Act,
the Election Commission:—

(A) appoints, with respect to the said election;

(a) the 05th March, 2026 (Thursday), as the last date for making nominations;
(b) the 06th March, 2026 (Friday), as the date for the scrutiny of the nominations;
(c) the 09th March, 2026 (Monday), as the last date for the withdrawal of candidatures;
(d) the 16th March, 2026 (Monday),as the date on which a poll shall, if necessary, be taken;
and
(e) the 20th March, 2026 (Friday), as the date before which the election shall be completed;
and

(B) fixes the hours from 09:00 AM to 04:00 PM, as the hours during which the poll shall, if
necessary, be taken in all the above mentioned States on the date specified above for the
election.
By order,

Sd/-
(SUMAN KUMAR DAS)
Secretary.
_________

ELECTION COMMISSION OF INDIA
Nirvachan Sadan, Ashoka Road, New Delhi – 110001

Dated: 26th February, 2026
Phalguna 07, 1947 (Saka)

NOTIFICATION

No. 318/CS-Multi/2026(2).—In pursuance of Section 21, and in exercise of the powers
conferred by sub-section (1) of Section 22, of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 (43 of

1951), the Election Commission, in consultation with the Government of each of the States,
specified in the column (1) of the TABLE below, hereby—

(i) designates the officer, specified in the corresponding entry in column (2) of the said
TABLE to be the Returning Officer; and

(ii) appoints the officer(s), specified in the corresponding entry in column (3) of the said
Table, to be the Assistant Returning Officer(s) to assist the Returning Officer in the
performance of his/her functions;
in respect of the biennial election to the Council of States, to be held in that State in pursuance of
the notification of the President of India, published in the Gazette of India on 26th February,
2026:—

25-02-2026 as required under Clause (3) of Article 348 of the Constitution of India].

URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT

NOTIFICATION
Shimla-2, the 25th February, 2026

No. UD-A(1)-13/2024.—WHEREAS, a proposal for declaration of Nagar Panchayat
Swarghat, District Bilaspur as notified vide this Department Notification No. UD-A(1)-13/2024,
dated 23-11-2024 was published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh on 25-11-2024 for
inviting objections from the affected persons through the Deputy Commissioner, Bilaspur within a
period of two weeks;

AND WHEREAS, after consideration of the objections received in this behalf, vide
Notification No. UD-A(1)-13/2024 dated, 20-12-2024 Nagar Panchayat Swarghat, District
Bilaspur, Himachal Pradesh was constituted as per the area specified in THE SCHEDULE
attached there to in accordance with the provisions of sub-section (2) of Section 3 and sub-section
(6) of Section 4 of the Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act, 1994 (State Act No. 13 of 1994), and the

land and buildings situated in the areas specified in THE SCHEDULE annexed to the said
Notification were exempted from payment of property tax for a period of three years under sub-
section (7) of Section 4 read with sub-section (1) of Section 80 of the said Act, while the residents
of the said areas continued to retain their customary rights as provided in the Wazib-ul-Urj of the
record of rights, and published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh on 24-12-2024;

AND WHEREAS, the Notification dated 20-12-2024 was challenged before the Hon’ble
High Court in Civil Writ Petition No. 2906 of 2025, titled as Bal Krishan & Others vs State of
Himachal Pradesh & others, and was quashed vide judgment dated 18-12-2025 with directions to
consider the objections of the petitioner(s) in accordance with law by passing a reasoned and
speaking order after affording an opportunity of personal hearing;
AND WHEREAS, in compliance with the aforesaid directions of the Hon’ble Court, the
objections were duly considered and finally decided by the Competent Authority vide order dated
09-02-2026 in accordance with law.
NOW THEREFORE, the Governor, Himachal Pradesh in exercise of the powers conferred
by sub-section (2) of Section (3) and sub-section (6) of Section 4 of the Himachal Pradesh
Municipal Act, 1994 (State Act No. 13 of 1994), is pleased to constitute Nagar Panchayat
Swarghat, District Bilaspur, Himachal Pradesh as per the area specified in THE SCHEDULE
here to, with effect from the date of publication of this Notification in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette),
Himachal Pradesh.

The Governor of Himachal Pradesh, further in exercise of the powers conferred by sub-
section (7) of Section 4 read with sub-section (1) of Section 80 of the Act ibid., is pleased to
exempt the land and buildings of the said Nagar Panchayat from the payment of property tax for
a period of three years from the commencement of this Notification. The residents of said areas
shall continue to retain their customary rights as provided in Wazib-Ul-Urj of the records of rights.

By order,
Sd/-

(DEVESH KUMAR),
Pr. Secretary (UD).

[Authoritative English text of this Department Notification No.UD-A(1)-12/2024 Dated
25-02-2026 as required under clause (3) of article 348 of the Constitution of India]

URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT

NOTIFICATION

Shimla-2, the 25th February, 2026

No.UD-A(1)-12/2024.—WHEREAS, a proposal for declaration of Nagar Panchayat
Jhandutta, District Bilaspur as notified vide this Department Notification No. UD-A(1)-12/2024
Dated 23-11-2024 was published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh on 25-11-2024 for
inviting objections from the affected persons through the Deputy Commissioner, Bilaspur within a
period of two weeks;

AND WHEREAS, after consideration of the objections received in this behalf, vide
Notification No UD-A(1)-12/2024 Dated 20-12-2024 Nagar Panchayat Jhandutta District Bilaspur
Himachal Pradesh was constituted as per the area specified in THE SCHEDULE attached thereto
in accordance with the provisions of sub-section (2) of Section 3 and sub-section (6) of Section 4 of
the Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act, 1994 (State Act No. 13 of 1994), and the land and buildings
situated in the areas specified in THE SCHEDULE annexed to the said Notification were
exempted from payment of property tax for a period of three years under sub-section (7) of Section
4 read with sub-section (1) of Section 80 of the said Act, while the residents of the said areas
continued to retain their customary rights as provided in the Wazib-ul-Urj of the record of rights,
and , published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh, on 24-12-2024;
AND WHEREAS, the Notification Dated 20-12-2024 was challenged before the Hon’ble
High Court in Civil Writ Petition No. 7978 of 2025, titled as Gian Chand & others vs State of
Himachal Pradesh & Ors, and was quashed vide judgment dated 06-10-2025 with directions to
consider the objections of the petitioner(s) in accordance with law by passing a reasoned and
speaking order after affording an opportunity of personal hearing;

AND WHEREAS, in compliance with the aforesaid directions of the Hon’ble Court, as
well as the directions issued by the Hon’ble Court in other similarly situated cases, including Civil
Writ Petition No. 2906/2025, 2709/2025 etc, the objections were duly considered and finally
decided by the Competent Authority vide order Dated 09-02-2026 in accordance with law.

NOW THEREFORE, the Governor, Himachal Pradesh, in exercise of the powers
conferred by sub-section (2) of Section (3) and sub-section (6) of Section 4 of the Himachal

Pradesh Municipal Act, 1994 (State Act No. 13 of 1994), is pleased to constitute Nagar Panchayat
Jhandutta, District Bilaspur, Himachal Pradesh as per the area specified in THE SCHEDULE
hereto, with effect from the date of publication of this Notification in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette),
Himachal Pradesh.

The Governor of Himachal Pradesh, further in exercise of the powers conferred by sub-
section (7) of Section 4 read with sub-section (1) of Section 80 of the Act ibid., is pleased to
exempt the land and buildings of the said Nagar Panchayat from the payment of property tax for
a period of three years from the commencement of this Notification. The residents of said areas
shall continue to retain their customary rights as provided in Wazib-Ul-Urj of the records of rights.

By Order,

Sd/-
(DEVESH KUMAR).
Pr. Secretary (UD).

[Authoritative English text of this Department Notification No. UD-A(1)-10/2024, dated
25-02-2026 as required under Clause (3) of Article 348 of the Constitution of India].
URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT

NOTIFICATION

Shimla-2, the 25th February, 2026

No. UD-A(1)-10/2024.—WHEREAS, a proposal for declaration of Nagar
Panchayat Kunihar, District Solan as notified vide this Department Notification No.
UD-A(1)-10/2024 dated 23-11-2024 was published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette),
Himachal Pradesh on 24-11-2024 for inviting objections from the affected persons
through the Deputy Commissioner, Solan within a period of two weeks;

AND WHEREAS, after consideration of the objections received in this behalf,
vide Notification No. UD-A(1)-10/2024, dated 23-12-2024 Nagar Panchayat
Kunihar, District Solan, Himachal Pradesh was constituted as per the area specified
in THE SCHEDULE attached thereto in accordance with the provisions of sub-section
(2) of Section 3 and sub-section (6) of Section 4 of the Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act,
1994 (State Act No. 13 of 1994), and the land and buildings situated in the areas specified
in THE SCHEDULE annexed to the said Notification were exempted from payment of
property tax for a period of three years under sub-section (7) of Section 4 read with sub-
section (1) of Section 80 of the said Act, while the residents of the said areas continued to
retain their customary rights as provided in the Wazib-ul-Urj of the record of rights and
published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh on 26-12-2024;

AND WHEREAS, the Notification Dated 23-12-2024 was challenged before the
Hon’ble High Court in Civil Writ Petition No. 2709 of 2025, titled as Kunihar Vikas Sabha
& Ors vs State of Himachal Pradesh & Ors, and was quashed vide judgment dated 18-12-
2025 with directions to consider the objections of the petitioner(s) in accordance with law
by passing a reasoned and speaking order after affording an opportunity of personal
hearing;

AND WHEREAS, in compliance with the aforesaid directions of the Hon’ble Court,
the objections were duly considered and finally decided by the Competent Authority vide
order dated 09-02-2026 in accordance with law.

NOW THEREFORE, the Governor, Himachal Pradesh, in exercise of the powers
conferred by sub-section (2) of Section (3) and sub-section (6) of Section 4 of the
Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act, 1994 (State Act No. 13 of 1994), is pleased to
constitute Nagar Panchayat Kunihar, District Solan, Himachal Pradesh as per the
area specified in THE SCHEDULE hereto, with effect from the date of publication of this
Notification in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh.

The Governor of Himachal Pradesh, further in exercise of the powers
conferred by sub- section (7) of Section 4 read with sub-section (1) of Section 80 of
the Act ibid, is pleased to exempt the land and buildings of the said Nagar
Panchayat from the payment of property tax for a period of three years from the
commencement of this notification. The residents of said areas shall continue to
retain their customary rights as provided in Wazib-Ul-Urj of the records of rights.

By Order,

(DEVESH KUMAR),
Pr. Secretary (UD).

[Authoritative English text of this Department Notification No. UD-A(1)-20/2024 dated
25-02-2026 as required under clause (3) of article 348 of the Constitution of India]
URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT

NOTIFICATION

Shimla-2, the 25th February, 2026
No. UD-A(1)-20/2024.—WHEREAS, a proposal for declaration of Nagar Panchayat
Nagrota Surian, District Kangra as notified vide this Department Notification No. UD-A(1)-
20/2024 dated 23-11-2024 was published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh on
24-11-2024 for inviting objections from the affected persons through the Deputy Commissioner,
Kangra within a period of two weeks;

AND WHEREAS, no objections were received within the stipulated period and accordingly
vide Notification No. UD-A(1)-20/2024 dated 20-12-2024 Nagar Panchayat Nagrota Surian
District Kangra, Himachal Pradesh was constituted as per the area specified in THE SCHEDULE
attached thereto in accordance with the provisions of sub-section (2) of Section 3 and sub-section
(6) of section 4 of the Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act, 1994 (Act No. 13 of 1994), and the land

and buildings situated in the areas specified in THE SCHEDULE annexed to the said Notification
were exempted from payment of property tax for a period of three years under sub-section (7) of 4
read with sub-section (1) of Section 80 of the said Act, while the residents of the said areas
continued to retain their customary rights as provided in the Wazib-ul-Urj of the record of rights,
and , published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh, on 26-12-2024;

AND WHEREAS, the Notification Dated 20-12-2024 was challenged before the Hon’ble
High Court in Civil Writ Petition No. 6074 of 2025, titled as Sanjay Mahajan & others vs State of
Himachal Pradesh & Ors, and was quashed vide judgment dated 19-11-2025 with directions to
consider the objections of the petitioner in accordance with law by passing a reasoned and speaking
order after affording an opportunity of personal hearing;

AND WHEREAS, in compliance with the aforesaid directions of the Hon’ble Court, as well
as the directions issued by the Hon’ble Court in other similarly situated cases, including Civil Writ
Petition No. 2906/2025, 2709/2025 etc, the objections were duly considered and finally decided
by the Competent Authority vide order dated 09-02-2026 in accordance with law.

NOW THEREFORE, the Governor, Himachal Pradesh, in exercise of the powers conferred
by sub-section (2) of Section (3) and sub-section (6) of Section 4 of the Himachal Pradesh
Municipal Act, 1994 (State Act No. 13 of 1994), is pleased to constitute Nagar Panchayat Nagrota
Surian District Kangra, Himachal Pradesh as per the area specified in THE SCHEDULE hereto,
with effect from the date of publication of this Notification in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal
Pradesh.

The Governor of Himachal Pradesh, further in exercise of the powers conferred by sub-
section (7) of section 4 read with sub-section (1) of section 80 of the Act ibid., is pleased to exempt
the land and buildings of the said Nagar Panchayat from the payment of property tax for a period
of three years from the commencement of this Notification. The residents of said areas shall
continue to retain their customary rights as provided in Wazib-Ul-Urj of the records of rights.

By order,

(DEVESH KUMAR),
Pr. Secretary (UD).

[Authoritative English text of this Department Notification No. UD-A(1)-5/2024 Dated
25-02-2026 as required under clause (3) of article 348 of the Constitution of India].
URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT

NOTIFICATION

Shimla-2, the 25th February, 2026
No. UD-A(1)-5/2024.—WHEREAS, a proposal for inclusion of some areas in Nagar
Panchayat, Nadaun, District Hamirpur in accordance with the provisions of sub-section (1) of
Section 5 of the Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act. 1994 (State Act. No. 13 of 1994) and to declare
Nagar Panchayat Nadaun as Municipal Council Nadaun as per second proviso to Section 3(2) of

the Act ibid was notified vide this Department Notification No. UD-A(1)-5/2024 Dated
23-11-2024 for inviting objections from the inhabitant(s)/person(s) likely to be affected thereby,
and published in Rajpatra (e- Gazette), Himachal Pradesh on 23-11-2024;

AND WHEREAS, after consideration of the objections received in this behalf, vide
Notification No. UD-A(1)-5/2024, Dated 20-12-2024, certain areas, as specified in the Schedule
appended thereto, were included within the limits of Nagar Panchayat Nadaun, District Hamirpur,
under sub-section (3) of Section 5 of the Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act, 1994 (State Act No. 13
of 1994); and Nagar Panchayat Nadaun was upgraded to Municipal Council Nadaun under the
second proviso to sub-section (2) of Section 3 of the said Act; and the land and buildings situated in
the said areas were exempted from payment of property tax for a period of three years under sub-
section 4 of section 5 read with sub-section (1) of Section 80 of the act ibid, while the residents of
the said areas continued to retain their customary rights as recorded in the Wazib-ul-Urj, as
published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh on 24-12-2024.

AND WHEREAS, a proposal for inclusion of some additional areas in proposed Municipal
Council, Nadaun, District Hamirpur was notified in accordance with the provisions of sub-
section (1) of section 5 of Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act. 1994 (State Act. No. 13 of 1994) for
inviting objections from the inhabitant(s)/person(s) likely to be affected thereby vide this
Department Notification No. UD-A(1)-5/2024-loose Dated 14-12-2024 and published in Rajpatra
(e- Gazette), Himachal Pradesh on 14-12-2024;

AND WHEREAS, no objections were received within the stipulated period in response to
the aforesaid Notification, and accordingly, vide Notification No. UD-A(1)-5/2024, Dated
30-12-2024, certain additional areas, as specified in the Schedule appended thereto, were included
within the limits of Municipal Council Nadaun, District Hamirpur, under sub-section (3) of Section
5 of the said Act, with the same statutory consequences as aforesaid, and the said Notification was
published in the Rajpatra on 31-12-2024;

AND WHEREAS, vide Notification No. UD-A(1)-5/2024, Dated 28-02-2025, a proposal
for inclusion of certain areas, as specified in the Schedule appended thereto, within the limits of
Municipal Council Nadaun, District Hamirpur, was notified under sub-section (1) of Section 5 of
the said Act, inviting objections from the inhabitants/persons likely to be affected thereby, and
published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh on 28-02-2025.

AND WHEREAS, the Notification Dated 20-12-2024 was challenged before the Hon’ble
High Court in Civil Writ Petition No. 936 of 2025, titled Pawan Kumar & others vs. State of
Himachal Pradesh & others, and was quashed vide judgment Dated 18-12-2025, with directions to
consider the objections of the petitioner(s) in accordance with law by passing a reasoned and
speaking order after affording an opportunity of personal hearing;

AND WHEREAS, in compliance with the directions of the Hon’ble High Court, the
objections on record, including those raised during personal hearing, have been duly considered
and finally decided by the Competent Authority vide order Dated 09-02-2026 in accordance with
law, and the present Notification is being issued strictly in terms of the said final decision.

NOW, THEREFORE, the Governor, Himachal Pradesh in exercise of the powers vested in
him under sub-section (3) of Section 5 of the Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act, 1994 (State Act
No. 13 of 1994), is pleased to include the areas specified in THE SCHEDULE attached with this
Notification in the Nagar Panchayat, Nadaun, District Hamirpur, Himachal Pradesh and, in exercise

of the powers conferred by the second proviso to sub-section (2) of Section 3 of the said Act, is
further pleased to upgrade Nagar Panchayat, Nadaun to that of Municipal Council, Nadaun, with
effect from the date of publication of this Notification in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal
Pradesh.

The Governor, Himachal Pradesh in exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (4)
of Section 5 read with sub-section (1) of Section 80 of Act ibid is further pleased to exempt the
land and buildings in the areas specified in THE SCHEDULE from payment of property tax
for a period of three years from the date of publication of this Notification in the Rajpatra (e-
Gazette), Himachal Pradesh. The residents of these areas shall continue to retain their customary
rights as provided in Wazib-Ul-Urj of the record of rights.
By order,

(DEVESH KUMAR),
Pr. Secretary (UD).

[Authoritative English Text of this Department Notification No. Ud-A(1)-2/2024
Dated 25-02-026 as Required under Clause (3) of Article 348 of the Constitution of India].
URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT

NOTIFICATION

Shimla-2, the 25th February, 2026
No. UD-A(1)-18/2024.—WHEREAS, a proposal for inclusion of some areas in Municipal
Council Jawalamukhi, District Kangra in accordance with the provisions of sub-section (1) of
Section 5 of the Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act. 1994 (State Act. No. 13 of 1994) was notified
vide this Department Notification No. UD-A(1)-18/2024, Dated 23-11-2024 for inviting objections
from the inhabitant(s)/person(s) likely to be affected thereby, and published in Rajpatra (e-
Gazette), Himachal Pradesh on 24-11-2024;

AND WHEREAS, after consideration of the objections received in this behalf, vide
Notification No. UD-A(1)-18/2024, dated 23-12-2024, certain areas, as specified in the Schedule
appended there to, were included within the limits of Municipal Council Jawalamukhi, District
Kangra, under sub-section (3) of Section 5 of the Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act, 1994 (State Act
No. 13 of 1994); and the land and buildings situated in the said areas were exempted from payment
of property tax for a period of three years; and the residents of the said areas continued to retain
their customary rights as recorded in the Wazib-ul-Urj, as published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette),
Himachal Pradesh on 26-12-2024.

AND WHEREAS, the Notification Dated 23-12-2024 was challenged before the Hon’ble
High Court in Civil Writ Petition Nos. 6313/2025-titled as Suman Lata vs State of Himachal

Pradesh & others and Civil Writ Petition No. 6319/2025-titeld as Ravi Chand vs State of Himachal
Pradesh & others and was quashed vide judgments dated 18-12-2025 with the directions to
consider the objections of the petitioner(s) in accordance with law by passing a reasoned and
speaking order after affording an opportunity of personal hearing;

AND WHEREAS, in compliance with the aforesaid directions of the Hon’ble Court, the
objections were duly considered and finally decided by the Competent Authority vide order dated
09-02-2026 in accordance with law.

NOW THEREFORE, the Governor, Himachal Pradesh, in exercise of the powers conferred
by sub-section (3) of Section 5 of the Himachal Pradesh Municipal Act, 1994 (State Act No. 13
of 1994), is pleased to include the area specified in THE SCHEDULE hereto, within the limits of
Municipal Council Jawalamukhi, District Kangra, Himachal Pradesh with effect from the date of
publication of this Notification in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh.

The Governor of Himachal Pradesh, further in exercise of the powers conferred by sub-
section (4) of Section 5 read with sub-section (1) of Section 80 of the Act ibid., is pleased to
exempt the land and buildings of the said areas from the payment of property tax for a period of
three years from the commencement of this Notification. The residents of said areas shall
continue to retain their customary rights as provided in Wazib-Ul-Urj of the records of rights.

By order,

(DEVESH KUMAR),
Pr. Secretary (UD).
